# Product: CLAROTY_XDOME
# Category: SaaS Applications
# Supported Format: SYSLOG , JSON , KV, CEF
# Reference: https://claroty.com/industrial-cybersecurity/xdome
# Copyright 2026 Google SecOps

# Custom Parser due to issues with actual parser
# Author - dominicchua@
# Last Updated: 2026-07-08


filter {
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "observer_hostname" => ""
      "version" => ""
      "kv_data" => ""
      "cat" => ""
      "client_id" => ""
      "device_asset_id" => ""
      "device_connection_type_list" => ""
      "device_ip_list" => ""
      "device_category" => ""
      "device_mac_list" => ""
      "device_manufacturer" => ""
      "device_model" => ""
      "device_network_list" => ""
      "device_os" => ""
      "device_site_name" => ""
      "device_subcategory" => ""
      "device_type" => ""
      "device_type_family" => ""
      "device_uid" => ""
      "dst" => ""
      "dstMAC" => ""
      "dstPort" => ""
      "src" => ""
      "srcMAC" => ""
      "srcPort" => ""
      "src_port" => ""
      "dst_port" => ""
      "geo_location" => ""
      "event_alert_id" => ""
      "event_description" => ""
      "event_id" => ""
      "server_port" => ""
      "intermediary" => ""
      "devTime" => ""
      "security_result" => ""
      "alert_category" => ""
      "alert_description" => ""
      "alert_device_status" => ""
      "alert_id" => ""
      "alert_mitre_technique_enterprise_ids" => ""
      "alert_mitre_technique_enterprise_names" => ""
      "alert_mitre_technique_ids" => ""
      "ip_list" => ""
      "firmware_original_versions" => ""
      "firmware_manufacturer" => ""
      "alert_name" => ""
      "event_vector" => ""
      "destination_printJobName" => ""
      "destination_printedFilesBackupPath" => ""
      "destination_printerName" => ""
      "source" => ""
      "private_ip" => ""
      "process_owner" => ""
      "risk_severity" => ""
      "msg_category" => ""
      "affected_device" => ""
      "vulnerability_device_info" => ""
      "vulnerability_info" => ""
      "comm_tuple" => ""
      "evnt_extra_info" => ""
      "affected_device_os" => ""
      "alert" => ""
      "alert_info" => ""
      "firmware_latest_known_versions" => ""
      "firmware_model" => ""
      "evnt_alert_id" => ""
      "evnt_type" => ""
      "evnt_id" => ""
      "evnt_timestamp" => ""
      "alert_mitre_technique_names" => ""
      "sub_category" => ""
      "change" => ""
      "evnt" => ""
      "group" => ""
      "signature_id" => ""
      "vulnerability_cves" => ""
      "app_protocol_output" => ""
      "no_network" => "true"
      "has_user" => "false"
      "affected_device_retired" => ""
      "management_ip" => ""
      "has_principal" => "false"
      "time" => ""
      "rule_name" => ""
      "signature_confidence_data" => ""
      "alert_info_signature_confidence" => ""
      "evnt_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_confidence" => ""
      "evnt_extra_info.ip_protocol" => ""
      "evnt_extra_info_geo_location" => ""
      "affected_device_data_available" => ""
      "device_name" => ""
      "device_note" => ""
      "device_retired" => ""
      "device_risk_score" => ""
      "device_app_version" => ""
      "device_vulnerability_relevance" => ""
      "device_vulnerability_relevance_source" => ""
      "device_vulnerability_overall_cvss_v3_score" => ""
      "device_vulnerability_manufacturer_remediation_info" => ""
      "device_vulnerability_manufacturer_remediation_info_source" => ""
      "vulnerability_name" => ""
      "vulnerability_type" => ""
      "vulnerability_note" => ""
      "vulnerability_affected_products" => ""
      "vulnerability_is_known_exploited" => ""
      "vulnerability_known_exploits" => ""
      "device_managed_device" => ""
      "vulnerability_release_date" => ""
      "alert_type_name" => ""
      "firmware_device_type" => ""
      "communication_type" => ""
      "policy_name" => ""
      "evnt_extra_info_direction" => ""
      "side_a_port" => ""
      "comm_tuple_protocol" => ""
      "affected_device_mac_list" => ""
      "sender_id" => ""
      "thumbprint" => ""
      "expiry_date" => ""
      "start_date" => ""
      "certificate_type" => ""
      "CN" => ""
      "OU" => ""
      "O" => ""
      "C" => ""
      "ST" => ""
      "L" => ""
      "affected_device_management_status" => ""
      "assignees" => ""
      "signature_content" => ""
      "side_a_ip" => ""
      "side_b_ip" => ""
      "side_a_mac" => ""
      "side_b_mac" => ""
      "side_b_port" => ""
      "mode" => ""
      "source_username" => ""
      "other_device_device_name" => ""
      "asset_id" => ""
      "uid" => ""
      "other_device_device_subcategory" => ""
      "site_name" => ""
      "manufacturer" => ""
      "management_status" => ""
      "other_device_os" => ""
      "other_device_device_type" => ""
      "other_device_device_type_family" => ""
      "other_device_risk_score" => ""
      "signature_severity_description" => ""
      "protocol" => ""
      "msg_content" => ""
      "flow" => ""
      "content" => ""
      "body_content" => ""
      "distance" => ""
      "reference" => ""
      "reference_url" => ""
      "classtype" => ""
      "sid" => ""
      "rev" => ""
      "metadata_description" => ""
      "device_category_data" => ""
      "policy_pair_label.value" => ""
      "has_extension_vulns" => "false"
      "signature_name" => ""
      "dst_ip" => ""
      "dst_mac" => ""
      "domain" => ""
      "src_ip" => ""
      "src_mac" => ""
      "evnt_description" => ""
      "direction" => ""
      "username" => ""
      "comm_tuple_src_mac" => ""
      "file_name" => ""
      "file_type" => ""
      "syslog_pid" => ""
    }
  }

  mutate {
    gsub => ["message","\\r\\n",""]
  }

  mutate {
    gsub => ["message","\\n",""]
  }

  mutate {
    gsub => ["message","@timestamp","timestamp"]
  }

  mutate {
    gsub => ["message","event","evnt"]
  }

  json {
    source => "message"
    array_function => "split_columns"
    on_error => "message_not_json"
  }

  mutate {
    gsub=> ["message","\\t","#"]
  }

  mutate {
    gsub=> ["message","□","#"]
  }

  grok {
    match => {
      "message" => [
        "<%{INT}>%{INT} %{TIMESTAMP_ISO8601:time} %{DATA:observer_hostname} %{DATA} %{INT} - - LEEF:%{DATA:version}\\|%{DATA}\\|%{DATA}\\|%{DATA}\\|%{DATA:msg_category}\\|%{GREEDYDATA:kv_data}",
        "<%{INT}>%{INT} %{TIMESTAMP_ISO8601:time} %{DATA:observer_hostname} %{DATA} %{INT} - - CEF:0\\|%{DATA}\\|%{DATA}\\|%{DATA}\\|.*?\\|%{DATA:msg_category}\\|%{INT}\\|%{GREEDYDATA:kv_data}",
        "<%{INT}> %{TIMESTAMP_ISO8601:time} %{DATA:observer_hostname} CEF:0\\|%{DATA}\\|%{DATA}\\|%{DATA}\\|.*?\\|%{DATA:msg_category}\\|%{INT}\\|%{GREEDYDATA:kv_data}",
        "<%{INT}>%{INT} %{TIMESTAMP_ISO8601:time} %{DATA:observer_hostname} %{DATA} %{INT:syslog_pid} - - %{GREEDYDATA:json_data}",
        "%{TIMESTAMP_ISO8601:time} %{DATA:observer_hostname} %{DATA}\\[%{INT:syslog_pid}\\] CEF:0\\|%{DATA}\\|%{DATA}\\|%{DATA}\\|.*?\\|%{DATA:msg_category}\\|%{INT}\\|%{GREEDYDATA:kv_data}",
        "%{TIMESTAMP_ISO8601:time} %{DATA:observer_hostname} CEF:0\\|%{DATA}\\|%{DATA}\\|%{DATA}\\|.*?\\|%{DATA:msg_category}\\|%{INT}\\|%{GREEDYDATA:kv_data}"
      ]
    }
    overwrite => ["observer_hostname", "version", "msg_category", "kv_data", "json_data", "time", "syslog_pid"]
    on_error => "no_grok"
  }

  if [message] =~ "CEF:0" {
    mutate {
      gsub => ["kv_data", " ([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)=", "#$1="]
    }
  }

  json {
    source => "json_data"
    array_function => "split_columns"
    on_error => "json_data_not_json"
  }

  kv {
    source => "kv_data"
    field_split => "#"
    value_split => "="
    on_error => "no_kv"
  }

  if [alert_type_name] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "alert_type_label.key" => "alert_type_name"
        "alert_type_label.value.string_value" => "%{alert_type_name}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_alert_type_name"
    }
    if ![invalid_alert_type_name] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "alert_type_label"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_alert_type_name_merge"
      }
    }
  }

  if [syslog_pid] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "syslog_pid_label.key" => "syslog_pid"
        "syslog_pid_label.value.string_value" => "%{syslog_pid}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_syslog_pid"
    }
    if ![invalid_syslog_pid] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "syslog_pid_label"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_syslog_pid_merge"
      }
    }
  }

  if [affected_device] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "principal_hostname" => "%{affected_device.device_name}"
      }
      on_error => "no_affected_device_device_name"
    }
    if ![no_affected_device_device_name] {
      grok {
        match => {
          "principal_hostname" => [
            "%{DATA:group}\\\\%{HOSTNAME:principal_hostname}"
          ]
        }
        overwrite => ["principal_hostname", "group"]
        on_error => "no_grok_hostname"
      }
      if [group] != "" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.administrative_domain" => "%{group}"
          }
          on_error => "no_group"
        }
      }
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "affected_device_risk_score_label.value" => "%{affected_device.risk_score}"
        "affected_device_risk_score_label.key" => "affected_device_risk_score"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_affected_device_risk_score_merge"
    }
    if ![invalid_affected_device_risk_score_merge] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.attribute.labels" => "affected_device_risk_score_label"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_affected_device_risk_score_label_merge"
      }
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "affected_device_app_version_label.value" => "%{affected_device.app_version}"
        "affected_device_app_version_label.key" => "affected_device_app_version"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_affected_device_app_version_merge"
    }
    if ![invalid_affected_device_app_version_merge] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.attribute.labels" => "affected_device_app_version_label"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_affected_device_app_version_merge_merge"
      }
    }

    for connectiontypelist in affected_device.connection_type_list {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "connection_type_list_label.value" => "%{connectiontypelist}"
          "connection_type_list_label.key" => "connection_type_list"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_connection_type_list"
      }
      if ![invalid_connection_type_list] {
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.attribute.labels" => "connection_type_list_label"
          }
          on_error => "invalid_connection_type_list_merge"
        }
      }
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "connection_type_list_label" => ""
        }
      }
    }

    mutate {
      convert => {
        "affected_device.retired" => "string"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_affected_device_retired_conversion"
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "affected_device_retired" => "%{affected_device.retired}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_affected_replace_error"
    }

    if [affected_device_retired] != "" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "affected_device_retired_label.value" => "%{affected_device_retired}"
          "affected_device_retired_label.key" => "affecteddeviceretired"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_affected_device_retired"
      }
      if ![invalid_affected_device_retired] {
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.attribute.labels" => "affected_device_retired_label"
          }
          on_error => "invalid_affected_device_retired_merge"
        }
      }
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "hardware.manufacturer" => "%{affected_device.manufacturer}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_device_manufacturer"
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "hardware.model" => "%{affected_device.model}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_device_model"
    }

    for networklist in affected_device.network_list {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "network_list_label.value" => "%{networklist}"
          "network_list_label.key" => "network_list"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_network_list"
      }
      if ![invalid_network_list] {
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.attribute.labels" => "network_list_label"
          }
          on_error => "invalid_network_list_merge"
        }
      }
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "affected_device_asset_id" => "%{affected_device.asset_id}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_affected_device_asset_id"
    }
    if ![invalid_affected_device_asset_id] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.asset_id" => "ASSET_ID:%{affected_device.asset_id}"
        }
        on_error => "asset_id_empty"
      }
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "affected_device_device_category" => "%{affected_device.device_category}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_affected_device_device_category"
    }
    if ![invalid_affected_device_device_category] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.category" => "%{affected_device_device_category}"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_affected_device_category"
      }
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "affected_device_device_subcategory" => "%{affected_device.device_subcategory}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_affected_device_device_subcategory"
    }
    if ![invalid_affected_device_device_subcategory] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "affected_device_device_subcategory_label.value" => "%{affected_device_device_subcategory}"
          "affected_device_device_subcategory_label.key" => "affected_device_device_subcategory"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_affected_device_subcategory"
      }
      if ![invalid_affected_device_subcategory] {
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.attribute.labels" => "affected_device_device_subcategory_label"
          }
          on_error => "invalid_affected_device_subcategory_merge"
        }
      }
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "affected_device_device_type" => "%{affected_device.device_type}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_affected_device_device_type"
    }
    if ![invalid_affected_device_device_type] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "affected_device_device_type_label.value" => "%{affected_device_device_type}"
          "affected_device_device_type_label.key" => "affected_device_device_type"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_affected_device_type"
      }
      if ![invalid_affected_device_type] {
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.attribute.labels" => "affected_device_device_type_label"
          }
          on_error => "invalid_affected_device_type_merge"
        }
      }
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "affected_device_device_type_family" => "%{affected_device.device_type_family}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_affected_device_device_type_family"
    }
    if ![invalid_affected_device_device_type_family] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "affected_device_device_type_family_label.value" => "%{affected_device_device_type_family}"
          "affected_device_device_type_family_label.key" => "affected_device_device_type_family"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_affected_device_type_family"
      }
      if ![invalid_affected_device_type_family] {
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.attribute.labels" => "affected_device_device_type_family_label"
          }
          on_error => "invalid_affected_device_type_family_merge"
        }
      }
    }

    for affectedip in affected_device.ip_list {
      mutate {
        split => {
          source => "affectedip"
          separator => " "
          target => "affectedips"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_affected_device_ip_list_split"
      }
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "affectedip" => "%{affectedips.0}"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_affected_device_ip_list"
      }
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "principal_ips" => "affectedip"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_affected_device_ip_list"
      }
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "affected_device_os" => "%{affected_device.os}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_affected_device_os"
    }
    mutate {
      lowercase => ["affected_device_os"]
      on_error => "affected_device_os_lowercase_failed"
    }

    if [affected_device_os] =~ "windows" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.platform_software.platform" => "WINDOWS"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_affected_device_os_windows"
      }
    }

    if [affected_device_os] =~ "linux" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.platform_software.platform" => "LINUX"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_affected_device_os_linux"
      }
    }

    if [affected_device_os] =~ "mac" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.platform_software.platform" => "MAC"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_affected_device_os_mac"
      }
    }

    else if [affected_device_os] != "" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "affected_device_os_label.value" => "%{affected_device_os}"
          "affected_device_os_label.key" => "affected_device_os"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_affected_device_os_label"
      }
      if ![invalid_affected_device_os_label] {
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.resource.attribute.labels" => "affected_device_os_label"
          }
          on_error => "affected_device_os_label_merge_empty"
        }
      }
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.namespace" => "%{affected_device.site_name}"
      }
      on_error => "no_location_name"
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "affected_device_uid" => "%{affected_device.uid}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_affected_device_uid"
    }
    if ![invalid_affected_device_uid] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.product_object_id" => "%{affected_device_uid}"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_affected_device_uid_merge"
      }
    }
  }

  if [alert] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "alertcategory" => "%{alert.category}"
      }
      on_error => "alertcategory_empty"
    }
    if ![alertcategory_empty] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "security_result.category_details" => "alertcategory"
        }
        on_error => "alertcategory_label_empty"
      }
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "alertdescription" => "%{alert.description}"
      }
      on_error => "alertdescription_empty"
    }
    if ![alertdescription_empty] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "security_result.description" => "%{alertdescription}"
        }
        on_error => "alertdescription_label_empty"
      }
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "alertdevice_status_label.key" => "alert_device_status"
        "alertdevice_status_label.value" => "%{alert.device_status}"
      }
      on_error => "alertdevice_status_label_empty"
    }
    if ![alertdevice_status_label_empty] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "security_result.detection_fields" => "alertdevice_status_label"
        }
        on_error => "alertdevice_status_label_empty"
      }
    }

    mutate {
      convert => {
        "alert.id" => "string"
      }
      on_error => "alert_id_conversion_failed"
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event_alert_id" => "%{alert.id}"
      }
      on_error => "alertid_label_empty"
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "alertname" => "%{alert.name}"
      }
      on_error => "alertname_empty"
    }
    if ![alertname_empty] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "rule_name" => "%{alertname}"
        }
        on_error => "alertname_label_empty"
      }
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "alert_type_name_label.key" => "type_name"
        "alert_type_name_label.value" => "%{alert.type_name}"
      }
      on_error => "no_alert_type_name"
    }
    if ![no_alert_type_name] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "security_result.detection_fields" => "alert_type_name_label"
        }
        on_error => "no_detection_fields"
      }
    }
  }

  if [alert_info] != "" {
    for index, alertinfomodel in alert_info.firmware_device_type {
      mutate {
        convert => {
          "index" => "string"
        }
      }
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "alert_info_firmware_device_type_label" => ""
        }
      }
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "alert_info_firmware_device_type_label.key" => "alert_info_firmware_device_type_%{index}"
          "alert_info_firmware_device_type_label.value.string_value" => "%{alertinfomodel}"
        }
        on_error => "alert_info_firmware_device_type_label_empty"
      }
      if ![alert_info_firmware_device_type_label_empty] {
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "alert_info_firmware_device_type_label"
          }
          on_error => "alert_info_firmware_device_type_label_merge_empty"
        }
      }
    }

    for index, latestversion in alert_info.firmware_latest_known_versions {
      mutate {
        convert => {
          "index" => "string"
        }
      }
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "alert_info_latest_software_version_label" => ""
        }
      }
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "alert_info_latest_software_version_label.key" => "alert_info_latest_software_version_%{index}"
          "alert_info_latest_software_version_label.value.string_value" => "%{latestversion.software_version}"
        }
        on_error => "alert_info_latest_software_version_label_empty"
      }
      if ![alert_info_latest_software_version_label_empty] {
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "alert_info_latest_software_version_label"
          }
          on_error => "alert_info_latest_software_version_label_merge_empty"
        }
      }
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "alert_info_firmware_manufacturer_label.key" => "alert_info_firmware_manufacturer"
        "alert_info_firmware_manufacturer_label.value" => "%{alert_info.firmware_manufacturer}"
      }
      on_error => "alert_info_firmware_manufacturer_label_empty"
    }
    if ![alert_info_firmware_manufacturer_label_empty] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.attribute.labels" => "alert_info_firmware_manufacturer_label"
        }
        on_error => "alert_info_firmware_manufacturer_label_merge_empty"
      }
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "alert_info_firmware_model_label.key" => "alert_info_firmware_model_%{index}"
        "alert_info_firmware_model_label.value.string_value" => "%{alert_info.firmware_model}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_alert_info_firmware_model"
    }
    if ![invalid_alert_info_firmware_model] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "alert_info_firmware_model_label"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_alert_info_firmware_model_merge"
      }
    }

    for index, originalversion in firmware_original_versions {
      mutate {
        convert => {
          "index" => "string"
        }
      }
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "firmware_original_versions" => ""
        }
      }
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "firmware_original_versions.key" => "alert_info_firmware_original_versions%{index}"
          "firmware_original_versions.value.string_value" => "%{originalversion.software_version}"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_firmware_original_versions"
      }
      if ![invalid_firmware_original_versions] {
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "firmware_original_versions"
          }
          on_error => "invalid_firmware_original_versions_merge"
        }
      }
    }

    mutate {
      convert => {
        "alert_info.signature_id" => "string"
      }
      on_error => "signature_id_conversion_failed"
    }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "signature_id" => "%{alert_info.signature_id}"
      }
      on_error => "signature_id_empty"
    }

    mutate {
      # rule_name is used if present later this reuses the same variable for
      # mapping to security_result.rule_name
      replace => {
        "rule_name" => "%{alert_info.signature_name}"
      }
      on_error => "signature_name_empty"
    }

    mutate {
      # risk_severity is used if present later this reuses the same variable for
      # mapping to security_result.severity

        "risk_severity" => "%{alert_info.signature_severity}"

      on_error => "signature_severity_not_found"

  }

  if [comm_tuple] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "comm_tuple_dst_ip" => "%{comm_tuple.dst_ip}"

      on_error => "comm_tuple_dst_ip_empty"

      mutate {

      }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "app_protocol_src" => "%{comm_tuple.protocol}"
      }
      on_error => "comm_tuple_protocol_empty"
    }
    if ![comm_tuple_protocol_empty] {
      # This file contains application protocol parsing logic
      # Interface:
      # app_protocol_src (required): string that contains application protocol or a
      #                              port number
      # app_protocol_output: string that is either empty or contains application
      #                      protocol compatible to ApplicationProtocol enum in
      #                      udm.proto

      mutate {
        uppercase => [ "app_protocol_src" ]
      }

      if [app_protocol_src] in [ "22", "SSH", "SSHD" ] {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "app_protocol_output" => "SSH"
          }
        }
      }
      else if [app_protocol_src] in [ "25", "SMTP" ] {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "app_protocol_output" => "SMTP"
          }
        }
      }
      else if [app_protocol_src] in [ "53", "DNS" ] {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "app_protocol_output" => "DNS"
          }
        }
      }
      else if [app_protocol_src] in [ "80", "8080", "HTTP" ] {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "app_protocol_output" => "HTTP"
          }
        }
      }
      else if [app_protocol_src] in [ "443", "HTTPS", "TLS" ] {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "app_protocol_output" => "HTTPS"
          }
        }
      }
      else if [app_protocol_src] in [ "7400", "7401", "7402", "DDS" ] {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "app_protocol_output" => "DDS"
          }
        }
      }
      else if [app_protocol_src] in [ "SMB", "SMBV1" ] {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "app_protocol_output" => "SMB"
          }
        }
      }
      else if [app_protocol_src] in [
        "UNKNOWN_APPLICATION_PROTOCOL", "AFP", "APPC", "AMQP", "ATOM", "BEEP",
        "BITCOIN", "BIT_TORRENT", "CFDP", "COAP", "DEVICE_NET", "DHCP", "E_DONKEY",
        "ENRP", "FAST_TRACK", "FINGER", "FREENET", "FTAM", "GOPHER", "HL7", "H323",
        "IRCP", "KADEMLIA", "LDAP", "LPD", "MIME", "MODBUS", "MQTT", "NETCONF",
        "NFS", "NIS", "NNTP", "NTCIP", "NTP", "OSCAR", "PNRP", "QUIC", "RDP", "RELP",
        "RIP", "RLOGIN", "RPC", "RTMP", "RTP", "RTPS", "RTSP", "SAP", "SDP", "SIP",
        "SLP", "SNTP", "SSMS", "STYX", "TCAP", "TDS", "TOR", "TSP", "VTP", "WHOIS",
        "WEB_DAV", "X400", "X500", "XMPP", "IEC104", "DCERPC"
      ] {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "app_protocol_output" => "%{app_protocol_src}"
          }
        }
      } else {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "app_protocol_output" => ""
          }
        }
      }
      # app_protocol_output is set here
    }
    
    mutate {
      convert => {
        "comm_tuple.server_port" => "string"
      }
      on_error => "comm_tuple_server_port_conversion_failed"
    }
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "comm_tuple_server_port" => "%{comm_tuple.server_port}"
      }
      on_error => "comm_tuple_server_port_empty"
    }
    if ![comm_tuple_server_port_empty] {

        on_error => "comm_tuple_server_port_merge_empty"
      }
      mutate {
        convert => {
          "intermediary.port" => "integer"
        }
        on_error => "comm_tuple_server_port_convert_failed"
      }
    }

    mutate {

          "principal_ips" => "comm_tuple_src_ip"

        on_error => "comm_tuple_src_ip_merge_empty"
      }
    }

      }
      on_error => "comm_tuple_src_mac_empty"
    }
    if ![comm_tuple_src_mac_empty] {
      grok {
        match => {
          "comm_tuple_src_mac" => "%{MAC:comm_tuple_src_mac}"
        }
        overwrite => ["comm_tuple_src_mac"]
        on_error => "comm_tuple_src_mac_empty"
      }
      if ![comm_tuple_src_mac_empty] {
        mutate {

            "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.mac" => "comm_tuple_src_mac"

    mutate {
      convert => {
        "comm_tuple.src_port" => "string"
      }
      on_error => "comm_tuple_src_port_conversion_failed"
    }
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "comm_tuple_src_port" => "%{comm_tuple.src_port}"
      }
      on_error => "comm_tuple_src_port_empty"
    }
    if ![comm_tuple_src_port_empty] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.port" => "%{comm_tuple_src_port}"
        }

        }
        on_error => "comm_tuple_src_port_convert_failed"
      }
    }

      grok {
        match => {
          "signature_content" => "alert http any any -> any any \\(msg:\\\"%{DATA:msg_content}\\\"; flow:%{DATA:flow}; http.uri; content:\\\"%{DATA:content}\\\"; startswith; fast_pattern; content:\\\"%{DATA:body_content}\\\"; distance:%{INT:distance}; reference:%{DATA:reference}; reference:%{DATA:reference_url}; classtype:%{DATA:classtype}; sid:%{DATA:sid}; rev:%{DATA:rev}; metadata:%{GREEDYDATA:metadata_description}"
        }
        overwrite => ["msg_content", "flow", "content", "body_content", "distance", "reference", "reference_url", "classtype", "sid", "rev", "metadata_description"]
        on_error => "invalid_signature_content"
      }
    }
    if [msg_content] != "" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "security_result.summary" => "%{msg_content}"
        }
        on_error => "msg_content_empty"
      }
    }
    if [flow] != "" {
      mutate {
        replace => {

          "flow_label.value" => "%{flow}"

      on_error => "manufacturer_empty"

    if [manufacturer] != "" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "manufacturer_label.key" => "manufacturer"
          "manufacturer_label.value" => "%{manufacturer}"
        }
        on_error => "manufacturer_label_empty"
      }
      if ![manufacturer_label_empty] {
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.target.resource.attribute.labels" => "manufacturer_label"
          }
          on_error => "manufacturer_label_merge_empty"
        }
      }
    }
    for index, connection_type in evnt_extra_info.other_device.connection_type_list {
      mutate {
        convert => {
          "index" => "string"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_evnt_extra_info_other_device_connection_type_list_convert"
      }
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "evnt_extra_info_other_device_connection_type_list_label" => ""
          "other_device_connection_type_list" => ""
        }
      }
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "evnt_extra_info_other_device_connection_type_list_label.key" => "other_device_connection_type_list_%{index}"
          "evnt_extra_info_other_device_connection_type_list_label.value.string_value" => "%{connection_type}"
        }
        on_error => "evnt_extra_info_other_device_connection_type_list_label_empty"
      }
      if ![evnt_extra_info_other_device_connection_type_list_label_empty] {
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "evnt_extra_info_other_device_connection_type_list_label"
          }
          on_error => "evnt_extra_info_other_device_connection_type_list_label_merge_empty"
        }
      }
    }
    for networklist in evnt_extra_info.other_device.network_list {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "other_device_network_list_label" => ""
          "network_list" => ""
        }
      }
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "other_device_network_list_label.value" => "%{networklist}"
          "other_device_network_list_label.key" => "network_list"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_other_device_network_list"
      }
      if ![invalid_other_device_network_list] {
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.target.resource.attribute.labels" => "other_device_network_list_label"
          }
          on_error => "invalid_other_device_network_list_merge"
        }

      on_error => "other_device_os_empty"

    if [other_device_os] != "" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "other_device_os_label.key" => "other_device_os"
          "other_device_os_label.value" => "%{other_device_os}"
        }
        on_error => "other_device_os_label_empty"
      }

            "event.idm.read_only_udm.target.resource.attribute.labels" => "other_device_os_label"
          }
          on_error => "other_device_os_label_merge_failed"
        }

          "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "start_date_label"

        on_error => "start_date_label_merge_empty"

    replace => {
      "CN" => "%{evnt_extra_info.certificate_info.CN}"
    }
    on_error => "CN_empty"

  if [CN] != "" {
   mutate {
      replace => {

        "CN_label.key" => "CN"
      }
      on_error => "CN_label_empty"
    }
    if ![CN_label_empty] {
      mutate {
        merge => {

  mutate {

      replace => {
        "OU_label.key" => "OU"

        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "OU_label"
        }
        on_error => "OU_label_merge_empty"

    }

        on_error => "ST_label_merge_empty"
      }
    }

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "L" => "%{evnt_extra_info.certificate_info.L}"
    }
    on_error => "L_empty"
  }
  if [L] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "L_label.key" => "L"
        "L_label.value.string_value" => "%{L}"

      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "L_label"
        }
        on_error => "L_label_merge_empty"
      }

  mutate {

      "file_name" => "%{evnt_extra_info.file_name}"

    on_error => "file_name_empty"

  if [file_name] != "" {
    mutate {
      merge => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.target.file.names" => "file_name"
      }
      on_error => "file_name_not_mapped"
    }
  }

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "file_type" => "%{evnt_extra_info.file_type}"
    }
    on_error => "file_type_empty"
  }
  if [file_type] not in ["", " "] {
    if [file_type] == "zip" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.target.file.file_type" => "FILE_TYPE_ZIP"
        }
      }
    }
    else if [file_type] == "exe" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.target.file.file_type" => "FILE_TYPE_DOS_EXE"
        }
      }
    }
    else if [file_type] == "pdf" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.target.file.file_type" => "FILE_TYPE_PDF"
        }
      }
    }
    else if [file_type] == "xlsx" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.target.file.file_type" => "FILE_TYPE_XLSX"
        }
      }
    }

  mutate {

      "sender_id" => "%{evnt_extra_info.sender_id}"

    on_error => "sender_id_empty"

  if [sender_id] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.user.userid" => "%{sender_id}"
      }
      on_error => "sender_id_replace_empty"
    }
  }

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "comm_tuple_protocol" => "%{comm_tuple.protocol}"
    }
    on_error => "invalid_comm_tuple_protocol"
  }
  if [comm_tuple_protocol] in ["FTP", "HTTP", "HTTPS", "CIP", "TLS", "SSH", "NTP", "DNS", "ICMP"] {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.network.application_protocol" => "%{comm_tuple_protocol}"
      }
      on_error => "comm_tuple_protocol_replace_empty"
    }
  }
  else if [app_protocol_output] == "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "app_protocol.key" => "protocol"
        "app_protocol.value.string_value" => "%{comm_tuple.protocol}"
      }
      on_error => "comm_tuple_protocol_empty"
    }
    if ![comm_tuple_protocol_empty] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "app_protocol"
        }
        on_error => "comm_tuple_protocol_merge_empty"
      }
    }
  }

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "protocol" => "%{comm_tuple.protocol}"
    }
    on_error => "protocol_not_found"
  }

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "protocol_number_src" => "%{ip_protocol}"
    }
    on_error => "protocol_number_src_empty"
  }
  if ![protocol_number_src_empty] {
    # This file contains the ip protocol convertor
    # Document:
    #   - https://www.iana.org/assignments/protocol-numbers/protocol-numbers.xhtml
    # Interface:
    # protocol_number_src (required): number with the value which represents the protocol
    # ip_protocol_out: string that is either empty or contains ip protocol
    #                  compatible to IpProtocol enum in udm.proto

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "ip_protocol_out" => "UNKNOWN_IP_PROTOCOL"
      }
    }

    if [protocol_number_src] in [ 1, "ICMP", "ICMPV6", 58 ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "ip_protocol_out" => "ICMP"
        }
      }
    } else if [protocol_number_src] in [ 2, "IGMP" ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "ip_protocol_out" => "IGMP"
        }
      }
    } else if [protocol_number_src] in [ 6, "TCP" ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "ip_protocol_out" => "TCP"
        }
      }
    } else if [protocol_number_src] in [ 17, "UDP" ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "ip_protocol_out" => "UDP"
        }
      }
    } else if [protocol_number_src] in [ 41, "IP6IN4" ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "ip_protocol_out" => "IP6IN4"
        }
      }
    } else if [protocol_number_src] in [ 47, "GRE" ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "ip_protocol_out" => "GRE"
        }
      }
    } else if [protocol_number_src] in [ 50, "ESP" ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "ip_protocol_out" => "ESP"
        }
      }
    } else if [protocol_number_src] in [ 88, "EIGRP" ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "ip_protocol_out" => "EIGRP"
        }
      }
    }  else if [protocol_number_src] in [ 97, "ETHERIP" ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "ip_protocol_out" => "ETHERIP"
        }
      }
    } else if [protocol_number_src] in [ 103, "PIM" ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "ip_protocol_out" => "PIM"
        }
      }
    } else if [protocol_number_src] in [ 112, "VRRP" ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "ip_protocol_out" => "VRRP"
        }
      }
    }

    if [ip_protocol_out] != "" and [ip_protocol_out] != "UNKNOWN_IP_PROTOCOL" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.network.ip_protocol" => "%{ip_protocol_out}"
        }
        on_error => "no_ip_protocol_out"
      }
    }
  }

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "protocol" => "%{protocol}"
    }
    on_error => "invalid_comm_tuple_protocol"
  }
  if [protocol] in ["FTP", "HTTP", "HTTPS", "CIP", "SSH", "NTP", "DNS", "ICMP"] {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.network.application_protocol" => "%{protocol}"
      }
      on_error => "comm_tuple_protocol_replace_empty"
    }
  }

  if [username] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.target.user.userid" => "%{username}"
        "has_user" => "true"
      }
      on_error => "username_replace_empty"
    }
  }

  if [protocol] == "TLS 1.2" {
   mutate {
      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.network.tls.version" => "%{protocol}"
      }
      on_error => "tls_version_replace_empty"
    }
  }

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "affected_device_management_status" => "%{affected_device.management_status}"

    on_error => "affected_device_management_status_empty"

  if [affected_device_management_status] != "" {
   mutate {
      replace => {
        "affected_device_management_status_label.value" => "%{affected_device_management_status}"
        "affected_device_management_status_label.key" => "affected_device_management_status"
      }
      on_error => "affected_device_management_status_label_empty"
    }
    if ![affected_device_management_status_label_empty] {

        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.resource.attribute.labels" => "affected_device_management_status_label"
        }
        on_error => "affected_device_management_status_label_merge_empty"
      }
    }
  }

  for index, assignee in affected_device.assignees {
    mutate {
      convert => {
        "index" => "string"
      }
      on_error => "index_conversion_failed"
    }
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "assignee_label" => ""
      }

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "assignee_label.value" => "%{assignee}"

  if [alert_type_name] != "" {

  }

  mutate {
    convert => {
      "evnt_extra_info.side_b_port" => "string"
    }
    on_error => "evnt_extra_info_side_b_port_conversion_failed"
  }
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "side_b_port" => "%{evnt_extra_info.side_b_port}"

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "other_device_device_type" => "%{evnt_extra_info.other_device.device_type}"

    on_error => "other_device_device_type_empty"

        "other_device_device_type_label.key" => "other_device_device_type"
        "other_device_device_type_label.value" => "%{other_device_device_type}"

      on_error => "other_device_device_type_label_empty"

    if ![other_device_device_type_label_empty] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.target.asset.attribute.labels" => "other_device_device_type_label"
        }
        on_error => "other_device_device_type_label_merge_empty"
      }
    }
  }

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "other_device_device_type_family" => "%{evnt_extra_info.other_device.device_type_family}"
    }
    on_error => "other_device_device_type_family_empty"
  }
  if [other_device_device_type_family] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "other_device_device_type_family_label.key" => "other_device_device_type_family"
        "other_device_device_type_family_label.value" => "%{other_device_device_type_family}"
      }
      on_error => "other_device_device_type_family_label_empty"
    }
    if ![other_device_device_type_family_label_empty] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.target.asset.attribute.labels" => "other_device_device_type_family_label"
        }
        on_error => "other_device_device_type_family_label_merge_empty"
      }
    }
  }

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "other_device_risk_score" => "%{evnt_extra_info.other_device.risk_score}"
    }
    on_error => "other_device_risk_score_empty"
  }
  if [other_device_risk_score] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "other_device_risk_score_label.key" => "other_device_risk_score"
        "other_device_risk_score_label.value" => "%{other_device_risk_score}"
      }
      on_error => "other_device_risk_score_label_empty"
    }
    if ![other_device_risk_score_label_empty] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.target.asset.attribute.labels" => "other_device_risk_score_label"
        }
        on_error => "other_device_risk_score_label_merge_empty"
      }
    }
  }

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "signature_severity_description" => "%{evnt_extra_info.ids_signature_info.signature_severity_description}"
    }
    on_error => "signature_severity_description_empty"
  }
  if [signature_severity_description] != "" {
  mutate {
      replace => {
        "signature_severity_description_label.key" => "signature_severity_description"
        "signature_severity_description_label.value" => "%{signature_severity_description}"
      }
      on_error => "signature_severity_description_label_empty"
    }
    if ![signature_severity_description_label_empty] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "security_result.detection_fields" => "signature_severity_description_label"
        }
        on_error => "signature_severity_description_label_merge_empty"
      }
    }
  }

  for data in affected_device.labels {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "value_array" => ""
      }
    }
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "value_array.string_value" => "%{data}"
      }
      on_error => "additional_data_empty"
    }
    mutate {
      merge => {
        "additional_data.value.list_value.values" => "value_array"
      }
      on_error => "additional_data_not_found"
    }
    if [value_array] != "" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "affected_device_data_available" => "true"
        }
      }
    }
  }
  if [affected_device_data_available] == "true" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "additional_data.key" => "affected_device_labels"
      }
    }
    mutate {
      merge => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "additional_data"
      }
      on_error => "additional_data_field_not_found"
    }
  }

  mutate {
    convert => {
      "evnt_extra_info.ids_signature_info.signature_active_rev" => "string"
    }
    on_error => "signature_active_rev_conversion_failed"
  }
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "event_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_active_rev" => "%{evnt_extra_info.ids_signature_info.signature_active_rev}"
    }
    on_error => "event_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_active_rev_empty"
  }
  if ![event_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_active_rev_empty] {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "security_result.rule_version" => "%{event_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_active_rev}"
      }
      on_error => "signature_active_rev_empty"
    }
  }

  mutate {
    convert => {
      "alert_info.signature_active_rev" => "string"
    }
    on_error => "signature_active_rev_conversion_failed"
  }
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "alert_info_signature_active_rev" => "%{alert_info.signature_active_rev}"
    }
    on_error => "alert_info_signature_active_rev_replace_empty"
  }
  if ![alert_info_signature_active_rev_replace_empty] {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "security_result.rule_version" => "%{alert_info_signature_active_rev}"
      }
      on_error => "alert_info_signature_active_rev_empty"
    }
  }

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "evnt_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_severity_description" => "%{evnt_extra_info.ids_signature_info.signature_severity_description}"
    }
    on_error => "evnt_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_severity_description_replace_error"
  }
  mutate {
    uppercase => ["evnt_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_severity_description"]
  }
  if ![evnt_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_severity_description_replace_error] {
    if [evnt_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_severity_description] =~ "CRITICAL" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "security_result.severity" => "CRITICAL"
        }
      }
    }
    else if [evnt_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_severity_description] =~ "HIGH" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "security_result.severity" => "HIGH"
        }
      }
    }
    else if [evnt_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_severity_description] =~ "MEDIUM" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "security_result.severity" => "MEDIUM"
        }
      }
    }
    else if [evnt_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_severity_description] =~ "LOW" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "security_result.severity" => "LOW"
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if [signature_active_rev_conversion_failed] {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "alert_info_signature_severity_description" => "%{alert_info.signature_severity_description}"
      }
      on_error => "alert_info_signature_severity_description_empty"
    }
    mutate {
      uppercase => ["alert_info_signature_severity_description"]
    }
    if ![alert_info_signature_severity_description_empty] {
      if [alert_info_signature_severity_description] =~ "CRITICAL" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "security_result.severity" => "CRITICAL"
          }
        }
      }
      else if [alert_info_signature_severity_description] =~ "HIGH" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "security_result.severity" => "HIGH"
          }
        }
      }
      else if [alert_info_signature_severity_description] =~ "MEDIUM" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "security_result.severity" => "MEDIUM"
          }
        }
      }
      else if [alert_info_signature_severity_description] =~ "LOW" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "security_result.severity" => "LOW"
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  else {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "alert_info_signature_severity_description.value"  => "%{alert_info.signature_severity_description}"
      }
      on_error => "alert_info_signature_severity_description_empty"
    }
    if ![alert_info_signature_severity_description_empty] and [alert_info_signature_severity_description][value] != "" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "alert_info_signature_severity_description.key" => "signature_severity_description"
        }
        on_error => "alert_info_signature_severity_description_key_empty"
      }
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "security_result.detection_fields" => "alert_info_signature_severity_description"
        }
        on_error => "alert_info_signature_severity_description_merge_empty"
      }
    }
  }

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "evnt_extra_info_geo_location" => "%{evnt_extra_info.geo_location}"
    }
    on_error => "evnt_extra_info_geo_location_empty"
  }
  if ![evnt_extra_info_geo_location_empty] and [evnt_extra_info_geo_location] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.location.city" => "%{evnt_extra_info_geo_location}"
      }
      on_error => "geo_location_empty"
    }
  }

  mutate {
    rename => {
      "affected_device.management_status" => "addition.management_status"
      "affected_device.note" => "addition.note"
      "evnt_extra_info.domain" => "addition.domain"
      "evnt_extra_info.src_domain" => "addition.src_domain"
      "evnt_extra_info.src_geo_location" => "addition.src_geo_location"
      "evnt_extra_info.ids_signature_info.signature_powered_by" => "addition.signature_powered_by"
      "evnt_extra_info.ids_signature_info.signature_first_released" => "addition.signature_first_released"
      "evnt_extra_info.ids_signature_info.signature_last_updated" => "addition.signature_last_updated"
      "evnt_extra_info.ids_signature_info.signature_last_updated_by_system" => "addition.signature_last_updated_by_system"
      "evnt_extra_info.other_device" => "addition.other_device"
      "alert.name" => "addition.name"
      "alert.note" => "addition.alert_note"
    }
  }

  for key, value in addition map {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "map_field" => ""
        "map_check" => ""
      }
    }
    mutate {
      convert => {
        "value" => "string"
      }
      on_error => "value_conversion_error"
    }
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "map_check" => "%{value}"
      }
      on_error => "no_value"
    }
    if ![no_value] and [map_check] != "" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "map_field.key" => "%{key}"
        }
        on_error => "key_replace_error"
      }
      if [key] in ["signature_first_released", "signature_last_updated", "signature_last_updated_by_system", "name" ] {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "map_field.value" => "%{value}"
          }
          on_error => "security_result_map_field_value_replace_error"
        }
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "security_result.detection_fields" => "map_field"
          }
          on_error => "security_result_addition_merge_failed"
        }
      }
      else {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "map_field.value.string_value" => "%{value}"
          }
          on_error => "additional_fields_map_field_value_replace_error"
        }
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "map_field"
          }
          on_error => "addition_merge_failed"
        }
      }
    }
  }

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "evnt_extra_info_ip_protocol" => "%{evnt_extra_info.ip_protocol}"
    }
    on_error => "evnt_extra_info_ip_protocol_replace_error"
  }
  if ![evnt_extra_info_ip_protocol_replace_error] {
    if [evnt_extra_info_ip_protocol] in ["EIGRP","ESP","ETHERIP","GRE","ICMP","IGMP","IP6IN4","PIM","TCP","UDP","VRRP"] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.network.ip_protocol" => "%{evnt_extra_info.ip_protocol}"
        }
        on_error => "ip_protocol_replace_error"
      }
    }
  }

  mutate {
    convert => {
      "evnt_extra_info.ids_signature_info.signature_confidence" => "string"
    }
    on_error => "signature_confidence_conversion_failed"
  }
  mutate {
    convert => {
      "alert_info.signature_confidence" => "string"
    }
    on_error => "signature_confidence_conversion_failed"
  }

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "alert_info_signature_confidence" => "%{alert_info.signature_confidence}"
    }
    on_error => "alert_info_signature_confidence_replace_failed"
  }
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "evnt_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_confidence" => "%{evnt_extra_info.ids_signature_info.signature_confidence}"
    }
    on_error => "evnt_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_confidence_replace_failed"
  }
  if [alert_info_signature_confidence] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "security_result.confidence_score" => "%{alert_info_signature_confidence}"
      }
      on_error => "alert_info_signature_confidence_replace_error"
    }
    mutate {
      convert => {
        "security_result.confidence_score" => "float"
      }
      on_error => "confidence_not_convert"
    }
  }
  if [evnt_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_confidence] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "security_result.confidence_score" => "%{evnt_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_confidence}"
      }
      on_error => "evnt_extra_info_ids_signature_info_signature_confidence_replace_error"
    }
    mutate {
      convert => {
        "security_result.confidence_score" => "float"
      }
      on_error => "confidence_not_convert"
    }
  }

  if [management_ip] != "" {
    grok {
      match => {
        "management_ip" => [
          "%{IP:management_ip}"
        ]
      }
      overwrite => ["management_ip"]
      on_error => "no_grok_management_ip"
    }
    if ![no_grok_management_ip] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.ip" => "management_ip"
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.ip" => "management_ip"
        }
        on_error => "no_principal_management_ip"
      }
      if ![no_principal_management_ip] {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "has_principal" => "true"
          }
          on_error => "no_has_principal"
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if [time] != "" {
    date {
      match => ["time","ISO8601", "RFC3339", "UNIX", "UNIX_MS"]
      on_error => "invalid_time"
    }
  }

  if [msg_category] != "" {
    if [cat] != "" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "sub_category" => "%{cat}"
        }
        on_error => "no_cat"
      }
    }
    if [evnt] != "" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "sub_category" => "%{evnt.type}"
        }
        on_error => "no_evnt"
      }
    }
    else if [alert] != "" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "sub_category" => "%{alert.type_name}"
        }
        on_error => "no_alert"
      }
    }
    else if [change] != "" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "sub_category" => "%{change.type}"
        }
        on_error => "no_change"
      }
    }
    else if [vulnerability_info] != "" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "sub_category" => "%{vulnerability_info.type}"
        }
        on_error => "no_vulnerability_info"
      }
    }
    if [msg_category] == "server_incident" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "sub_category" => "%{incident_type}"
        }
        on_error => "no_incident_type"
      }
    }

    if [sub_category] != "" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.metadata.product_event_type" => "%{msg_category}: %{sub_category}"
        }
        on_error => "msg_category_empty"
      }
    } else {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.metadata.product_event_type" => "%{msg_category}"
        }
        on_error => "msg_category_empty1"
      }
    }
  }

  if [vulnerability_device_info] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "vulnerability_device_info_relevance" => "%{vulnerability_device_info.relevance}"
      }
      on_error => "vulnerability_device_info_relevance_empty"
    }
    if ![vulnerability_device_info_relevance_empty] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "vulnerability_device_info_relevance_label.value" => "%{vulnerability_device_info_relevance}"
          "vulnerability_device_info_relevance_label.key" => "vulnerability_device_info_relevance"
        }
        on_error => "vulnerability_device_info_relevance_label_empty"
      }
      if ![vulnerability_device_info_relevance_label_empty] {
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "security_result.detection_fields" => "vulnerability_device_info_relevance_label"
          }
          on_error => "vulnerability_device_info_relevance_label_merge_empty"
        }
      }
    }

    mutate {
      convert => {
        "vulnerability_device_info.overall_cvss_v3_score" => "string"
      }
      on_error => "vulnerability_device_info_overall_cvss_v3_conversion_failed"
    }
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "vulnerability_device_info_overall_cvss_v3_score_label.value" => "%{vulnerability_device_info.overall_cvss_v3_score}"
        "vulnerability_device_info_overall_cvss_v3_score_label.key" => "vulnerability_device_info_overall_cvss_v3_score"
      }
      on_error => "vulnerability_device_info_overall_cvss_v3_score_label_empty"
    }
    if ![vulnerability_device_info_overall_cvss_v3_score_label_empty] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "security_result.detection_fields" => "vulnerability_device_info_overall_cvss_v3_score_label"
        }
        on_error => "vulnerability_device_info_overall_cvss_v3_score_label_merge_empty"
      }
    }

    for index, relevance_source in vulnerability_device_info.relevance_source {
      mutate {
        convert => {
          "index" => "string"
        }
      }
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "vulnerability_device_info_relevance_source_label.value" => "%{relevance_source}"
          "vulnerability_device_info_relevance_source_label.key" => "vulnerability_device_info_relevance_source_%{index}"
        }
        on_error => "vulnerability_device_info_relevance_source_label_empty"
      }
      if ![vulnerability_device_info_relevance_source_label_empty] {
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "security_result.detection_fields" => "vulnerability_device_info_relevance_source_label"
          }
          on_error => "vulnerability_device_info_relevance_source_label_merge_empty"
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if [vulnerability_cves] != "" {
    mutate {
      gsub => ["vulnerability_cves","'",'"']
    }
    mutate {
      replace => {
          "vulnerability_cves" => "{\"vulnerability_info\": {\"cves\":\%{vulnerability_cves}}\}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_cves_list"
    }
    json {
      source => "vulnerability_cves"
      array_function => "split_columns"
      on_error => "no_cves_list"
    }
    mutate {
      copy => {
        "vulnerability_info.description" => "vulnerability_description"
      }
    }
  }

  grok {
    match => {
      "vulnerability_info.description" => "The advisory contains the following cves - ;%{GREEDYDATA:vulnerability_info_description}"
    }
    overwrite => ["vulnerability_info.description"]
    on_error => "one_vulnerability"
  }

  if [one_vulnerability] {
    mutate {
      split => {
        source => "vulnerability_info.description"
        separator => "ignore_me_this_creates_an_arrary"
        target => "vulnerability_info_description"
      }
      on_error => "no_vulnerability_info_description"
    }
  } else {
    mutate {
      split => {
        source => "vulnerability_info.description"
        separator => ";"
        target => "vulnerability_info_description"
      }
      on_error => "no_vulnerability_info_description1"
    }
  }

  for i, desc in vulnerability_info_description {
    grok {
      match => {
        "desc" => "(?P<dcve>CVE\\-\\d{4}\\-\\d+) - %{GREEDYDATA:cvedescription} ?"
      }
      overwrite => ["dcve", "cvedescription"]
      on_error => "no_match"
    }
    for index, cve in vulnerability_info.cves {
      # if the key doesn't exist the for loop will silently fail
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "vulnerability" => ""
        }
      }
      if (![no_match] && [dcve] == [cve]) || [one_vulnerability] {
        # LEEF format
        if [one_vulnerability] {
          mutate {
            replace => {
              "vulnerability.description" => "%{vulnerability_info_description.0}"
            }
            on_error => "no_vulnerability_description"
          }
        } else {
          mutate {
            replace => {
              "vulnerability.description" => "%{cvedescription}"
            }
            on_error => "no_vulnerability_description1"
          }
        }
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "vulnerability.cve_id" => "%{cve}"
          }
          on_error => "no_vulnerability_cve_id"
        }
        # if the first rename works the second will fail because the field
        # cvss_base_score already exists
        mutate {
          rename => {
            "vulnerability_info.cvssv3_base_score" => "vulnerability.cvss_base_score"
          }
          replace => {
            "vulnerability.cvss_version" => "3"
          }
          on_error => "no_vulnerability_cvssv3_base_score"
        }
        mutate {
          rename => {
             "vulnerability_info.cvssv2_base_score" => "vulnerability.cvss_base_score"
          }
          replace => {
            "vulnerability.cvss_version" => "2"
          }
          on_error => "no_vulnerability_cvssv2_base_score"
        }
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "cvssv2_base_score" => "%{vulnerability_cvssv2_base_score}"
          }
          convert => {
            "cvssv2_base_score" => "float"
          }
          on_error => "no_vulnerability_cvssv2_base_score"
        }
        if ![no_vulnerability_cvssv2_base_score] {
          mutate {
            copy => {
              "vulnerability.cvss_base_score" => "cvssv2_base_score"
            }
            replace => {
              "vulnerability.cvss_version" => "2"
            }
            on_error => "no_vulnerability_cvssv2_base_score_copy"
          }
        }
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "cvssv3_base_score" => "%{vulnerability_cvssv3_base_score}"
          }
          convert => {
            "cvssv3_base_score" => "float"
          }
          on_error => "no_vulnerability_cvssv3_base_score"
        }
        if ![no_vulnerability_cvssv3_base_score]{
          mutate {
            copy => {
              "vulnerability.cvss_base_score" => "cvssv3_base_score"
            }
            replace => {
              "vulnerability.cvss_version" => "3"
            }
            on_error => "no_vulnerability_cvssv3_base_score_copy"
          }
        }
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "vulnerabilities" => "vulnerability"
          }
          on_error => "vulnerability_merge_error"
        }
        if ![vulnerability_merge_error] {
          mutate {
            replace => {

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "app_protocol_src" => "%{proto}"

  if ![app_protocol_src_empty] {
    # This file contains application protocol parsing logic
    # Interface:
    # app_protocol_src (required): string that contains application protocol or a
    #                              port number
    # app_protocol_output: string that is either empty or contains application
    #                      protocol compatible to ApplicationProtocol enum in
    #                      udm.proto

    mutate {
      uppercase => [ "app_protocol_src" ]
    }

    if [app_protocol_src] in [ "22", "SSH", "SSHD" ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "app_protocol_output" => "SSH"
        }
      }
    }
    else if [app_protocol_src] in [ "25", "SMTP" ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "app_protocol_output" => "SMTP"
        }
      }
    }
    else if [app_protocol_src] in [ "53", "DNS" ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "app_protocol_output" => "DNS"
        }
      }
    }
    else if [app_protocol_src] in [ "80", "8080", "HTTP" ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "app_protocol_output" => "HTTP"
        }
      }
    }
    else if [app_protocol_src] in [ "443", "HTTPS", "TLS" ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "app_protocol_output" => "HTTPS"
        }
      }
    }
    else if [app_protocol_src] in [ "7400", "7401", "7402", "DDS" ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "app_protocol_output" => "DDS"
        }
      }
    }
    else if [app_protocol_src] in [ "SMB", "SMBV1" ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "app_protocol_output" => "SMB"
        }
      }
    }
    else if [app_protocol_src] in [
      "UNKNOWN_APPLICATION_PROTOCOL", "AFP", "APPC", "AMQP", "ATOM", "BEEP",
      "BITCOIN", "BIT_TORRENT", "CFDP", "COAP", "DEVICE_NET", "DHCP", "E_DONKEY",
      "ENRP", "FAST_TRACK", "FINGER", "FREENET", "FTAM", "GOPHER", "HL7", "H323",
      "IRCP", "KADEMLIA", "LDAP", "LPD", "MIME", "MODBUS", "MQTT", "NETCONF",
      "NFS", "NIS", "NNTP", "NTCIP", "NTP", "OSCAR", "PNRP", "QUIC", "RDP", "RELP",
      "RIP", "RLOGIN", "RPC", "RTMP", "RTP", "RTPS", "RTSP", "SAP", "SDP", "SIP",
      "SLP", "SNTP", "SSMS", "STYX", "TCAP", "TDS", "TOR", "TSP", "VTP", "WHOIS",
      "WEB_DAV", "X400", "X500", "XMPP", "IEC104", "DCERPC"
    ] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "app_protocol_output" => "%{app_protocol_src}"
        }
      }
    } else {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "app_protocol_output" => ""
        }
      }
    }
    # app_protocol_output is set here
  }

  if [server_port] != "" && [server_port] != "None" {

    mutate {
      replace => {
        "policy_pair_arr.string_value" => "%{value}"
      }
      on_error => "policy_pair_arr_failed"
    }
    if ![policy_pair_arr_failed] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "policy_pair_label.value.list_value.values" => "policy_pair_arr"
        }
        on_error => "policy_pair_arr_merge_failed"
      }
    }
  }
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "policy_pair_label.key" => "policy_pair"
    }
  }
  if [policy_pair_label][value] != "" {
    mutate {
      merge => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "policy_pair_label"
      }
      on_error => "policy_pair_label_merge_failed"
    }
  }

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "policy_name" => "%{evnt_extra_info.policy_info.policy_name}"
    }
    on_error => "policy_name_empty"
  }
  if [policy_name] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "security_result.rule_name" => "%{policy_name}"
      }
      on_error => "policy_name_replace_error"
    }
  }

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "communication_type" => "%{evnt_extra_info.communication_type}"
    }
    on_error => "communication_type_empty"
  }
  if [communication_type] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "communication_type_label.key" => "communication_type"
        "communication_type_label.value" => "%{communication_type}"
      }
      on_error => "communication_type_label_empty"
    }
    if ![communication_type_label_empty] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "security_result.detection_fields" => "communication_type_label"
        }
        on_error => "communication_type_label_merge_empty"
      }
    }
  }

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "evnt_extra_info_direction" => "%{evnt_extra_info.direction}"
    }
    on_error => "evnt_extra_info_direction_empty"
  }
  if ![evnt_extra_info_direction_empty] {
    if [evnt_extra_info_direction] == "From A to B" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.network.direction" => "OUTBOUND"
        }
      }
    } else if [evnt_extra_info_direction] == "From B to A" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.network.direction" => "INBOUND"
        }
      }
    } else if [evnt_extra_info_direction] == "Bidirectional" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "evnt_extra_info_direction_label.key" => "direction"
          "evnt_extra_info_direction_label.value.string_value" => "%{evnt_extra_info_direction}"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_evnt_extra_info_direction"
      }
      if ![invalid_evnt_extra_info_direction] {
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "evnt_extra_info_direction_label"
          }
          on_error => "merge_evnt_extra_info_direction_failed"
        }
      }
    }
  }

  mutate {
    convert => {
      "evnt_extra_info.side_a_port" => "string"
    }
    on_error => "evnt_extra_info_side_a_port_convert_error"
  }
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "side_a_port" => "%{evnt_extra_info.side_a_port}"
    }
    on_error => "side_a_port_empty"
  }
  if [side_a_port] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "side_a_port_label.key" => "side_a_port"
        "side_a_port_label.value.string_value" => "%{side_a_port}"

    if ![side_a_port_label_empty] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "side_a_port_label"
        }
        on_error => "side_a_port_label_merge_failed"
      }
    }
  }

   # convert LEEF to json
  if [alert_mitre_technique_names] != "" {
    mutate {
      gsub => ["alert_mitre_technique_names","'",'"']
    }
    mutate {
      replace => {
          "alert_mitre_technique_names" => "{\"alert\":{\"mitre_technique_names\":\%{alert_mitre_technique_names}\}}"
      }
      on_error => "alert_mitre_technique_names_replace_error"
    }
    json {
      source => "alert_mitre_technique_names"
      array_function => "split_columns"
      on_error => "alert_mitre_technique_names_split_columns_error"
    }
  }

  # convert LEEF to json
  if [alert_mitre_technique_ids] != "" {
    mutate {
      gsub => ["alert_mitre_technique_ids","'",'"']
    }
    mutate {
      replace => {
          "alert_mitre_technique_ids" => "{\"alert\":{\"mitre_technique_ids\":\%{alert_mitre_technique_ids}\}}"
      }
      on_error => "alert_mitre_technique_ids_replace_error"
    }
    json {
      source => "alert_mitre_technique_ids"
      array_function => "split_columns"
      on_error => "alert_mitre_technique_ids_split_columns_error"
    }
  }

  # these techniques are non enterprise
  for index,mitre_technique_id in alert.mitre_technique_ids {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "technique_name" => ""
        "technique_data" => ""
      }
    }
    # there is no way to use the index on alert.mitre_technique_names
    if index == 0 {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "technique_name" => "%{alert.mitre_technique_names.0}"
        }
        on_error => "technique_name_replace_error"
      }
    } else if index == 1 {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "technique_name" => "%{alert.mitre_technique_names.1}"
        }
        on_error => "technique_name_replace_error1"
      }
    } else if index == 2 {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "technique_name" => "%{alert.mitre_technique_names.2}"
        }
        on_error => "technique_name_replace_error2"
      }
    }
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "technique_data.id" => "%{mitre_technique_id}"
        "technique_data.name" => "%{technique_name}"
      }
      on_error => "technique_data_replace_error"
    }

    if [technique_data] != "" {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "security_result.attack_details.techniques" => "technique_data"
        }
        on_error => "technique_data_merge_error"
      }
    }
  }

  # convert LEEF to json
  if [alert_mitre_technique_enterprise_ids] != "" {
    mutate {
      gsub => ["alert_mitre_technique_enterprise_ids","'",'"']

    mutate {

    }
  }
