    else if [technique_data][id] in ["T1020","T1030","T1048","T1041","T1011","T1052","T1567","T1029","T1537"] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "tactics_data.id" => "TA0010"
          "tactics_data.name" => "Exfiltration"
        }
      }
    }
    else if [technique_data][id] in ["T1531","T1485","T1486","T1565","T1491","T1561","T1499","T1495","T1490","T1498","T1496","T1489","T1529"] {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "tactics_data.id" => "TA0040"
          "tactics_data.name" => "Impact"
        }
      }
    }
    else if [technique_data][id] == "" and [subtechnique_id] == "" {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "technique_data" => ""
        }
      }
    }

    if [technique_data] == "" {
      if [tactic_id] == "TA0043" or [tactic_name] == "Reconnaissance" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "tactics_data.id" => "TA0043"
            "tactics_data.name" => "Reconnaissance"
          }
        }
      }
      if [tactic_id] == "TA0042" or [tactic_name] == "Resource Development" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "tactics_data.id" => "TA0042"
            "tactics_data.name" => "Resource Development"
          }
        }
      }
      if [tactic_id] == "TA0001" or [tactic_name] == "Initial Access" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "tactics_data.id" => "TA0001"
            "tactics_data.name" => "Initial Access"
          }
        }
      }
      if [tactic_id] == "TA0002" or [tactic_name] == "Execution" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "tactics_data.id" => "TA0002"
            "tactics_data.name" => "Execution"
          }
        }
      }
      if [tactic_id] == "TA0003" or [tactic_name] == "Persistence" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "tactics_data.id" => "TA0003"
            "tactics_data.name" => "Persistence"
          }
        }
      }
      if [tactic_id] == "TA0004" or [tactic_name] == "Privilege Escalation" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "tactics_data.id" => "TA0004"
            "tactics_data.name" => "Privilege Escalation"
          }
        }
      }
      if [tactic_id] == "TA0005" or [tactic_name] == "Defense Evasion" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "tactics_data.id" => "TA0005"
            "tactics_data.name" => "Defense Evasion"
          }
        }
      }
      if [tactic_id] == "TA0006" or [tactic_name] == "Credential Access" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "tactics_data.id" => "TA0006"
            "tactics_data.name" => "Credential Access"
          }
        }
      }
      if [tactic_id] == "TA0007" or [tactic_name] == "Discovery" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "tactics_data.id" => "TA0007"
            "tactics_data.name" => "Discovery"
          }
        }
      }
      if [tactic_id] == "TA0008" or [tactic_name] == "Lateral Movement" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "tactics_data.id" => "TA0008"
            "tactics_data.name" => "Lateral Movement"
          }
        }
      }
      if [tactic_id] == "TA0009" or [tactic_name] == "Collection" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "tactics_data.id" => "TA0009"
            "tactics_data.name" => "Collection"
          }
        }
      }
      if [tactic_id] == "TA0011" or [tactic_name] == "Command and Control" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "tactics_data.id" => "TA0011"
            "tactics_data.name" => "Command and Control"
          }
        }
      }
      if [tactic_id] == "TA0010" or [tactic_name] == "Exfiltration" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "tactics_data.id" => "TA0010"
            "tactics_data.name" => "Exfiltration"
          }
        }
      }
      if [tactic_id] == "TA0040" or [tactic_name] == "Impact" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "tactics_data.id" => "TA0040"
            "tactics_data.name" => "Impact"
          }
        }
      }
    }

    if [technique_data] != "" {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "security_result.attack_details.techniques" => "technique_data"
        }
        on_error => "technique_data_merge_error"
      }
    }
    if [tactics_data] != "" {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "security_result.attack_details.tactics" => "tactics_data"
        }
        on_error => "tactics_data_merge_error1"
      }
    }
  }

  if [firmware_manufacturer] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "firmware_manufacturer_label.key" => "firmware_manufacturer"
        "firmware_manufacturer_label.value" => "%{firmware_manufacturer}"
      }
      on_error => "firmware_manufacturer_label_empty"
    }
    if ![firmware_manufacturer_label_empty] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.attribute.labels" => "firmware_manufacturer_label"
        }
        on_error => "firmware_manufacturer_label_merge_empty"
      }
    }
  }
  if [alert_name] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "rule_name" => "%{alert_name}"
      }
      on_error => "alert_name_label_empty"
    }
  }
  if [signature_name] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "signature_name_label.key" => "signature_name"
        "signature_name_label.value.string_value" => "%{signature_name}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_signature_name"
    }
    if ![invalid_signature_name] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "signature_name_label"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_signature_name_merge"
      }
    }
  }
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "event_action" => "%{evnt.action}"
    }
    on_error => "invalid_evnt_action"
  }
  if ![invalid_evnt_action] {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "evnt_action_label.key" => "evnt_action"
        "evnt_action_label.value.string_value" => "%{evnt_action}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_evnt_action_replace"
    }
    if ![invalid_evnt_action_replace] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "evnt_action_label"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_evnt_action_merge"
      }

      "evnt_description" => "%{evnt.description}"

    on_error => "invalid_evnt_description"

        "event.idm.read_only_udm.metadata.description" => "%{evnt_description}"

      on_error => "invalid_evnt_description_replace"
    }
  }
  mutate {
    convert => {
      "evnt.id" => "string"

    on_error => "invalid_evnt_id_convert"
  }
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "evnt_id" => "%{evnt.id}"
    }
    on_error => "invalid_evnt_id"

      on_error => "invalid_evnt_id_replace"

    convert => {
      "evnt.alert_id" => "string"

    on_error => "invalid_evnt_alert_id_convert"
  }
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "event_alert_id" => "%{evnt.alert_id}"
    }
    on_error => "invalid_evnt_alert_id"
  }
  # overwrite security_result.rule_id if signature_id is present
  if [signature_id] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "security_result.rule_id" => "%{signature_id}"
      }
      on_error => "signature_id_replace_error"
    }
  }
  if [rule_name] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "security_result.rule_name" => "%{rule_name}"
      }
      on_error => "rule_name_replace_error"
    }
  }
  date {
    match => ["evnt.ingested","ISO8601"]
    target => "event.idm.read_only_udm.metadata.ingested_timestamp"
    on_error => "invalid_evnt_ingested"
  }
  date {
    match => ["evnt.inserted","yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.SSSSSSSSSZ","ISO8601"]
    on_error => "invalid_evnt_inserted"
  }
  date {
    match => ["timestamp","ISO8601","yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.SSSZ"]
    target => "event.idm.read_only_udm.metadata.event_timestamp"
    on_error => "invalid_timestamp"
  }
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "event_observer" => "%{evnt.observer}"
    }
    on_error => "event_observer_not_found"
  }
  if ![event_observer_not_found]{
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event_observer_label.key" => "event_observer"
        "event_observer_label.value" => "%{event_observer}"
      }
      on_error => "event_observer_not_mapped"
    }
    if ![event_observer_not_mapped] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.observer.labels" => "event_observer_label"
        }
        on_error => "event_observer_merge_failed"
      }
    }
  }
  date {
    match => ["evnt.timestamp","RFC3339", "UNIX", "ISO8601", "UNIX_MS"]
    target => "event.idm.read_only_udm.metadata.event_timestamp"
    on_error => "invalid_evnt_timestamp"
  }
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "event_vector" => "%{evnt.vector}"
    }
    on_error => "event_vector_not_found"

  if ![event_vector_not_found] && [event_vector] not in ["None",""] {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event_vector_label.key" => "event_vector"
        "event_vector_label.value" => "%{event_vector}"
      }
      on_error => "event_vector_not_mapped"
    }
    if ![event_vector_not_mapped] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "security_result.detection_fields" => "event_vector_label"
        }
        on_error => "event_vector_merge_failed"

      on_error => "no_file_changeType"

    if ![no_file_changeType] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "security_result.about.resource.attribute.labels" => "file_changeType_label"

  mutate {

  if ![destination_printJobName_not_found] && [destination_printJobName] not in ["None", ""] {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "destination_printJobName_label.key" => "destination_printJobName"
        "destination_printJobName_label.value.string_value" => "%{destination_printJobName}"
      }
      on_error => "destination_printJobName_not_mapped"

    if ![destination_printJobName_not_mapped] {

        on_error => "destination_printJobName_merge_failed"
      }
    }
  }
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "destination_printedFilesBackupPath" => "%{destination.printedFilesBackupPath}"
    }
    on_error => "destination_printedFilesBackupPath_not_found"
  }
  if ![destination_printedFilesBackupPath_not_found] && [destination_printedFilesBackupPath] not in ["None", ""] {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "destination_printedFilesBackupPath_label.key" => "destination_printedFilesBackupPath"
        "destination_printedFilesBackupPath_label.value.string_value" => "%{destination_printedFilesBackupPath}"
      }
      on_error => "destination_printedFilesBackupPath_not_mapped"
    }
    if ![destination_printedFilesBackupPath_not_mapped] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "destination_printedFilesBackupPath_label"
        }
        on_error => "destination_printedFilesBackupPath_merge_failed"
      }
    }
  }
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "destination_printerName" => "%{destination.printerName}"
    }
    on_error => "destination_printerName_not_found"
  }
  if ![destination_printerName_not_found] && [destination_printerName] not in ["None", ""] {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "destination_printerName_label.key" => "destination_printerName"
        "destination_printerName_label.value.string_value" => "%{destination_printerName}"
      }
      on_error => "destination_printerName_not_mapped"
    }
    if ![destination_printerName_not_mapped] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "destination_printerName_label"
        }
        on_error => "destination_printerName_merge_failed"
      }
    }
  }
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "process_owner" => "%{process.owner}"
    }
    on_error => "process_owner_not_found"
  }
  if ![process_owner_not_found] && [process_owner] not in ["None",""] {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "process_owner_label.key" => "process_owner"
        "process_owner_label.value.string_value" => "%{process_owner}"
      }
      on_error => "process_owner_not_mapped"
    }
    if ![process_owner_not_mapped] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "process_owner_label"
        }
        on_error => "process_owner_merge_failed"
      }
    }
  }
  for index,indicator in risk.indicators {
    mutate {
      convert => {
        "index" => "string"
      }
    }
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "risk_indicator_id_label" => ""
        "risk_indicator_name_label" => ""
        "risk_indicator_weight_label" => ""
      }
    }
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "risk_indicator_id_label.key" => "risk_indicator_id_%{index}"
        "risk_indicator_id_label.value" => "%{indicator.id}"
      }
      on_error => "indicator_label_not_mapped"
    }
    if ![indicator_label_not_mapped] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "security_result.detection_fields" => "risk_indicator_id_label"
        }
        on_error => "indicator_label_merge_failed"
      }
    }
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "risk_indicator_name_label.key" => "risk_indicator_name_%{index}"
        "risk_indicator_name_label.value" => "%{indicator.name}"
      }
      on_error => "indicator_label_not_mapped"
    }
    if ![indicator_label_not_mapped] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "security_result.detection_fields" => "risk_indicator_name_label"
        }
        on_error => "indicator_label_merge_failed"
      }
    }
    mutate {
      convert => {
        "indicator.weight" => "string"
      }
      on_error => "indicator_weight_conversion_failed"
    }
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "risk_indicator_weight_label.key" => "risk_indicator_weight_%{index}"
        "risk_indicator_weight_label.value" => "%{indicator.weight}"
      }
      on_error => "indicator_label_not_mapped"
    }
    if ![indicator_label_not_mapped] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "security_result.detection_fields" => "risk_indicator_weight_label"
        }
        on_error => "indicator_label_merge_failed"
      }
    }
  }
  mutate {
    convert => {
      "risk.score" => "string"
    }
    on_error => "risk_score_string_conversion_failed"
  }
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "security_result.risk_score" => "%{risk.score}"
    }
    on_error => "risk_score_not_found"
  }
  mutate {
    convert => {
      "security_result.risk_score" => "float"
    }
    on_error => "risk_score_conversion_failed"
  }
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "risk_severity" => "%{risk.severity}"
    }
    on_error => "risk_severity_not_found"
  }
  mutate {
    uppercase => ["risk_severity"]
    on_error => "risk_severity_not_mapped"
  }
  if [risk_severity] =~ "LOW" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "security_result.severity" => "LOW"
      }
    }
  }
  if [risk_severity] =~ "MEDIUM" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "security_result.severity" => "MEDIUM"
      }
    }
  }
  if [risk_severity] =~ "HIGH" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "security_result.severity" => "HIGH"
      }
    }
  }
  if [source] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "source_ip" => "%{source.ip}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_source_ip"
    }
    if ![invalid_source_ip] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "principal_ips" => "source_ip"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_source_ip_merge"
      }
    }
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "source_operatingSystem" => "%{source.operatingSystem}"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_source_operatingSystem"
    }
    if ![invalid_source_operatingSystem] && [source_operatingSystem] != "" {
      mutate {
        lowercase => ["source_operatingSystem"]
        on_error => "invalid_source_operatingSystem_lowercase"
      }
      if [source_operatingSystem] =~ "windows" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.platform_software.platform" => "WINDOWS"
          }
        }

      if [source_operatingSystem] =~ "linux" {

            "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.platform_software.platform" => "LINUX"
          }
        }
      }
      if [source_operatingSystem] =~ "mac" {
        mutate {
          replace => {
            "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.platform_software.platform" => "MAC"
          }
        }
      }
    }
    for privateip in source.privateIp {
      mutate {
        replace => {
          "ips" => "%{privateip}"
        }
        on_error => "invalid_private_ip"
      }
      if ![invalid_private_ip] && [ips] != "" {
        mutate {
          merge => {
            "principal_ips" => "ips"
          }
          on_error => "invalid_private_ip_merge"
        }

  mutate {

    on_error => "user_deviceUid_not_found"

  if ![user_deviceUid_not_found] && [user_deviceUid] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.asset_id" => "deviceUid: %{user_deviceUid}"
      }

  mutate {

  if ![user_email_not_found] && [user_email] != "" {

      on_error => "user_email_replace_failed"

    if ![user_email_replace_failed] {
      mutate {

  mutate {
    replace => {
      "user_id" => "%{user.id}"

    on_error => "user_id_not_found"

  if ![user_id_not_found] && [user_id] != "" {

      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.user.product_object_id" => "%{user_id}"
      }
      on_error => "user_id_replace_failed"
    }
  }
  grok {
    match => {
      "user.actorHour" => "%{USER}/%{TIMESTAMP_ISO8601:ts}"
    }
    overwrite => ["ts"]
    on_error => "user_actorHour_grok_failed"
  }
  if ![user_actorHour_grok_failed] {
    date {
      match => ["ts","ISO8601"]
      on_error => "user_actorHour_date_failed"
    }
  }

  if [intermediary] != "" {
    mutate {
      merge => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.intermediary" => "intermediary"
      }
      on_error => "invalid_intermediary"
    }
  }
  if [security_result] != "" {
    mutate {
      merge => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.security_result" => "security_result"
      }
    }
  }
  mutate {
    replace => {

      "event.idm.read_only_udm.metadata.vendor_name" => "Claroty"

  }
  mutate {
    replace => {
      "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.hostname" => "%{principal_hostname}"

    on_error => "no_observer_hostname"
  }
  if [app_protocol_output] != "" {
    mutate {

        "event.idm.read_only_udm.network.application_protocol" => "%{app_protocol_output}"
      }
      on_error => "no_network"
    }
  } else {
    mutate {
      # just force setting no_network
      convert => {
        "no_network" => "boolean"
      }
      on_error => "no_network"
    }
  }
  mutate {
    copy => {
      "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.ip" => "principal_ips"
      "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.ip" => "principal_ips"
    }
    on_error => "no_principal_ip"
  }
  mutate {
    copy => {
      "event.idm.read_only_udm.target.ip" => "target_ips"
      "event.idm.read_only_udm.target.asset.ip" => "target_ips"
    }
    on_error => "no_target_ip"
  }
  if [event_alert_id] != ""{
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "add_alert_id.key" => "alert_id"
        "add_alert_id.value.string_value" => "%{event_alert_id}"
      }
      on_error => "alert_id_not_found"
    }
    if ![alert_id_not_found] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "add_alert_id"
        }
        on_error => "alert_id_merge_failed"
      }
    }
  }
  if [evnt_alert_id] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "security_result.rule_id" => "%{evnt_alert_id}"
      }
      on_error => "event_alert_id_sr_rule_id"
    }
  }
  if [evnt_id] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.metadata.product_log_id" => "%{evnt_id}"
      }
      on_error => "event_id_not_found"
    }
  }
  if [evnt_timestamp] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "evnt_timestamp_label.key" => "event_timestamp"
        "evnt_timestamp_label.value.string_value" => "%{evnt_timestamp}"
      }
      on_error => "evnt_timestamp_not_found"
    }
    if ![evnt_timestamp_not_found] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "evnt_timestamp_label"
        }
        on_error => "evnt_timestamp_merge_failed"
      }
    }
  }
  if [evnt_description] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.metadata.description" => "%{evnt_description}"
      }
      on_error => "evnt_description_not_found"
    }
  }
  mutate {
    uppercase => ["direction"]
    on_error => "direction_not_found"
  }
  if [direction] == "INBOUND" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.network.direction" => "INBOUND"
      }
    }
  }
  else if [direction] == "OUTBOUND" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.network.direction" => "OUTBOUND"
      }
    }
  }
  else if [direction] != "" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "direction_label.key" => "direction"
        "direction_label.value.string_value" => "%{direction}"
      }
      on_error => "direction_not_found"
    }
    if ![direction_not_found] {
      mutate {
        merge => {
          "event.idm.read_only_udm.additional.fields" => "direction_label"
        }
        on_error => "direction_merge_failed"
      }
    }
  }
  mutate {
    copy => {
      "event.idm.read_only_udm.extensions.vulns.vulnerabilities" => "vulnerabilities"
    }
    on_error => "no_vulnerabilities"
  }
  mutate {
    merge => {
      "event.idm.read_only_udm.principal.asset.hardware" => "hardware"
    }
    on_error => "hardware_merge_failed"
  }
  if ![no_network] and ![no_principal_ip] and ![no_target_ip] {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.metadata.event_type" => "NETWORK_CONNECTION"
      }
    }
  } else if [msg_category] == "vulnerability_affected_device" and [has_extension_vulns] == "true" and (![no_principal_ip] or [has_principal] == "true") {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.metadata.event_type" => "SCAN_VULN_HOST"
      }
    }
  } else if (![no_principal_hostname] and ![no_principal_ip]) && [has_user] == "true" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.metadata.event_type" => "USER_UNCATEGORIZED"
      }
    }
  }
  else if (![no_principal_hostname] || ![no_principal_ip]) or [has_principal] == "true" {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.metadata.event_type" => "STATUS_UPDATE"
      }
    }
  }
  else {
    mutate {
      replace => {
        "event.idm.read_only_udm.metadata.event_type" => "GENERIC_EVENT"
      }
    }
  }
  mutate {
    merge => {
      "@output" => "event"
    }
  }
}
